## 上一轮交接 (task_context)

### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
- 本项目只能用 westb-52439 的 cuda:0 和 cuda:1（项目配置白名单）
- 模型目录用 `olmo-1b-hf`（不是 `olmo-1b`），之前踩过坑
- Qwen2.5 系列不能用（代码占比过高，LoRA 无法覆盖，MVP 阶段已验证两次失败）
- submit_training_job 的 command 必须自带环境激活：`source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && cd /root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est && bash scripts/run_pipeline_xxx.sh`

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
- 服务器：westb-52439（2× RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell 98GB），account: root
- 工作目录：`/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est`
- Python：`/root/miniconda3/bin/python`（conda base 环境，不是 venv）
- 模型路径：`/root/autodl-tmp/models/`（pythia-1.4b/, gpt2-xl/, olmo-1b-hf/）
- 数据路径：`/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/data/depth_0.jsonl`（5000 条 Wikipedia）
- 日志目录：`/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/logs/`
- Checkpoint 目录：`/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/checkpoints/`
- 活跃进程：cross_model_gpt2xl_001 (PID 123650, cuda:1, tmux 管理)

### 3. 📊 当前状态
**我刚做完**：收到 pythia_temp09_001 CRON 完成回调，派了两个 Worker：
1. `worker_analysis_pythia_temp09_001_20260514T163125` — SSH 读日志提取 metrics，与 baseline 对比
2. `worker_submit_cross_model_olmo_002_20260514T163144` — 准备在 cuda:0 提交 OLMo-1B 重跑

**正在等待**：两个 Worker 的 [WORKER DONE] 回调均未返回。已向 Director 汇报了初步状态。

**Registry 状态**：
| exp_id | 状态 | GPU | 说明 |
|--------|------|-----|------|
| pythia_temp09_001 | ANALYZING | cuda:0(完成) | temp-only τ=0.9，Worker 正在提取 metrics |
| cross_model_gpt2xl_001 | RUNNING | cuda:1 | GPT-2 XL pipeline，预计 ~19:00 UTC 完成 |
| cross_model_olmo_002 | PREPARING | — | Worker 正在准备提交到 cuda:0 |
| pythia_topk50_001 | PREPARING | — | 等 cuda:1（GPT-2 XL 完成后） |

**还收到了一条重复 CRON 回调**（pythia_temp09_001 第二次），无需处理。

### 4. 📋 待办
- analysis Worker 回调 → 读 metrics → update_registry(conclusion, metrics) → 更新 report.md → 向 Director 详细汇报（含 vs baseline 对比 + 反思实验计划 + 文献联动）
- OLMo Worker 回调 → 确认提交成功 → 更新 ROADMAP 状态
- cross_model_gpt2xl_001 CRON 完成回调 → 派 Worker 分析 → cuda:1 释放 → 提交 pythia_topk50_001 (plan_001c)
- plan_001b + plan_002 + plan_003 全部完成 → 可启动 plan_004 (DCOL) 和 plan_006 (Atlas)
- 如果 pythia_temp09_001 conclusion = negative → 按 red-section skill 产出 Framing 调整提案，禁止直接跑下一个

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
- **D001**：MVP PASS — Pythia-1.4B nucleus p=0.95，4-class acc 78.2%，3-class (d1-d3) 70.8%。递归深度可区分。
- **D002**：Dream 审稿完成，Best=3.0/Median=3.0/Worst=2.5。Atlas/EDD 为 primary contribution，DCOL 为 secondary。6 项结构性必修已写入 paper_framing.md。
- **核心 baseline 指标**：4-class acc 78.2%, filtered 81.6%, 3-class 70.8%, d1v2 AUC 0.861, top features = var_surprisal/p90_ppl/mean_ppl
- **易错点**：pythia_temp09 的 hypothesis 是"跨解码策略泛化"——如果 metrics 与 nucleus 接近（±5pp），这本身就是 success（证明策略无关性）。不要因为没有"显著提升"就判 negative。
- **pipeline 结构**：每个实验是 3 轮 LoRA finetune + 3 轮 generate + feature extraction + classification 的完整管线，wall clock ~1.5-2h per pipeline
- **GPU 预算**：P0 预估 45 GPU·h (~1 day on 2 GPUs)，P0+P1 共 83 GPU·h

    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

\"notes\": \"## Comparison: temp_only τ=0.9 vs baseline nucleus p=0.95\\n\\n### 4-class classification (d0-d3)\\n| Metric | Baseline (nucleus p=0.95) | Temp09 (τ=0.9) | Δ | -0.0007 |\\n| AUC 1v2 | 0.8607 | 0.8327 | -0.028 |\\n| AUC 2v3 | 0.8005 | 0.7928 | -0.008 |\\n\\n### 3-class classification (d1-d3 only)\\n| Metric | Baseline | Temp09 | Δ |\\n|--------|----------|--------|---|\\n| Full acc | 70.8% | 69.83% | -1.0pp |\\n| Filtered acc | — | 72.52% [70.6, 74.3] | — |\\n| Filtered d1v2 AUC | — | 0.8656 | — |\\n| Filtered d2v3 AUC | — | 0.8295 | — |\\n\\n### Feature importance (top-5, same ranking)\\n1. var_surprisal (0.178 vs 0.169)\\n2. p90_ppl (0.150 vs 0.158)\\n3. mean_ppl (0.114 vs 0.120)\\n4. mean_surprisal (0.089 vs 0.088)\\n5. p75_ppl (0.069 vs 0.067)\\n\\n### Code contamination\\n| Depth | Baseline | Temp09 | Δ |\\n|-------|----------|--------|---|\\n| d1 | 5.0% | 7.7% | +2.7pp |\\n| d2 | 9.0% | 11.6% | +2.6pp |\\n| d3 | 12.0% | 13.6% | +1.6pp |\\n\\n### Analysis\\n1. **Depth signal robust across decoding strategies**: temp_only τ=0.9 achieves 76.25% 4-class acc (vs 78.15% baseline), only 1.9pp lower — well within the hypothesis that depth signal is decoding-strategy-agnostic.\\n2. **Ordinal structure preserved**: AUC ordering 0v1 > 1v3 > 1v2 > 2v3 identical to baseline, confirming the difficulty gradient d0≫d1>d2>d3 is robust.\\n3. **d2 remains the weak link**: d2 recall (0.554 in 3-class) is the lowest, consistent with baseline — d2 is the hardest to distinguish from both d1 and d3.\\n4. **Feature importance stable**: Top-5 features identical between strategies with near-identical importance weights, suggesting the same statistical signatures carry depth information regardless of decoding method.\\n5. **Higher code contamination**: Temp09 shows 2-3pp higher code-like content across depths, possibly because temperature-only sampling without top-p truncation preserves more of the distribution tail including code patterns. The filtered 3-class acc (72.52%) mitigates this.\\n6. **Conclusion: SUCCESS** — 4-class acc 76.25% >> 40% threshold, d1v2 AUC 0.8327 >> 0.6 threshold. p90_ppl (0.150 vs 0.158)\\\\n3. mean_ppl (0.114 vs 0.120)\\\\n4. mean_surprisal (0.089 vs 0.088)\\\\n5. p75_ppl (0.069 vs 0.067)\\\\n\\\\n### Code contamination\\\\n| Depth | Baseline | Temp09 | Δ |\\\\n|-------|----------|--------|---|\\\\n| d1 | 5.0% | 7.7% | +2.7pp |\\\\n| d2 | 9.0% | 11.6% | +2.6pp |\\\\n| d3 | 12.0% | 13.6% | +1.6pp |\\\\n\\\\n### Analysis\\\\n1. **Depth signal robust across decoding strategies**: temp_only τ=0.9 achieves 76.25% 4-class acc (vs 78.15% baseline), only 1.9pp lower — well within the hypothesis that depth signal is decoding-strategy-agnostic.\\\\n2. **Ordinal structure preserved**: AUC ordering 0v1 > 1v3 > 1v2 > 2v3 identical to baseline, confirming the difficulty gradient d0≫d1>d2>d3 is robust.\\\\n3. **d2 remains the weak link**: d2 recall (0.554 in 3-class) is the lowest, consistent with baseline — d2 is the hardest to distinguish from both d1 and d3.\\\\n4. **Feature importance stable**: Top-5 features identical between strategies with near-identical importance weights, suggesting the same statistical signatures carry depth information regardless of decoding method.\\\\n5. **Higher code contamination**: Temp09 shows 2-3pp higher code-like content across depths, possibly because temperature-only sampling without top-p truncation preserves more of the distribution tail including code patterns. The filtered 3-class acc (72.52%) mitigates this.\\\\n6. **Conclusion: SUCCESS** — 4-class acc 76.25% >> 40% threshold, d1v2 AUC 0.8327 >> 0.6 threshold. \"notes\": \"## Comparison: temp_only τ=0.9 vs baseline nucleus p=0.95\\n\\n### 4-class classification (d0-d3)\\n| Metric | Baseline (nucleus p=0.95) | Temp09 (τ=0.9) | Δ |\\n|--------|--------------------------|-----------------|---|\\n| RF acc | 78.15% [76.9, 79.4] | 76.25% [74.9, 77.5] | -1.9pp |\\n| LR acc | 74.92% | 74.05% | -0.9pp |\\n| AUC 0v1 | 0.9975 | 0.9968 | -0.0007 |\\n| AUC 1v2 | 0.8607 | 0.8327 | -0.028 |\\n| AUC 2v3 | 0.8005 | 0.7928 | -0.008 |\\n\\n### 3-class classification (d1-d3 only)\\n| Metric | Baseline | Temp09 | Δ |\\n|--------|----------|--------|---|\\n| Full acc | 70.8% | 69.83% | -1.0pp |\\n| Filtered acc | — | 72.52% [70.6, 74.3] | — |\\n| Filtered d1v2 AUC | — | 0.8656 | — |\\n| Filtered d2v3 AUC | — | 0.8295 | — |\\n\\n### Feature importance (top-5, same ranking)\\n1. var_surprisal (0.178 vs 0.169)\\n2. p90_ppl (0.150 vs 0.158)\\n3. mean_ppl (0.114 vs 0.120)\\n4. mean_surprisal (0.089 vs 0.088)\\n5. p75_ppl (0.069 vs 0.067)\\n\\n### Code contamination\\n| Depth | Baseline | Temp09 | Δ |\\n|-------|----------|--------|---|\\n| d1 | 5.0% | 7.7% | +2.7pp |\\n| d2 | 9.0% | 11.6% | +2.6pp |\\n| d3 | 12.0% | 13.6% | +1.6pp |\\n\\n### Analysis\\n1. **Depth signal robust across decoding strategies**: temp_only τ=0.9 achieves 76.25% 4-class acc (vs 78.15% baseline), only 1.9pp lower — well within the hypothesis that depth signal is decoding-strategy-agnostic.\\n2. **Ordinal structure preserved**: AUC ordering 0v1 > 1v3 > 1v2 > 2v3 identical to baseline, confirming the difficulty gradient d0≫d1>d2>d3 is robust.\\n3. **d2 remains the weak link**: d2 recall (0.554 in 3-class) is the lowest, consistent with baseline — d2 is the hardest to distinguish from both d1 and d3.\\n4. **Feature importance stable**: Top-5 features identical between strategies with near-identical importance weights, suggesting the same statistical signatures carry depth information regardless of decoding method.\\n5. **Higher code contamination**: Temp09 shows 2-3pp higher code-like content across depths, possibly because temperature-only sampling without top-p truncation preserves more of the distribution tail including code patterns. The filtered 3-class acc (72.52%) mitigates this.\\n6. **Conclusion: SUCCESS** — 4-class acc 76.25% >> 40% threshold, d1v2 AUC 0.8327 >> 0.6 threshold. Hypothesis confirmed: temperature-only decoding preserves recursive depth signal.\", \"completed_at\": \"2026-05-14T16:42:54.633Z\"}, \"mvp_pipeline_pythia_002\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", _source line 29 · 2026-05-14T19:35:17.561Z_

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01W6i4HRjxESx8bgEJuKw1th`</summary>

```text
1	---
2	name: resource-saturation
3	description: "当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。"
4	always: false
5	roles:
6	  - director
7	  - agent
8	---
9	
10	# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）
11	
12	## A. GPU 分配率检查
13	
14	调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:
15	- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号
16	- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）
17	- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️
18	
19	任何"分配了但没跑"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:
20	- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备
21	- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）
22	- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报"暂无可铺实验"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作
23	
24	**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。
25	
26	## B. GPU 效率检查
27	
28	调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:
29	- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务
30	- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）
31	- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优
32	
33	## C. 非 GPU 任务并行
34	
35	GPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:
36	- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint
37	- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置
38	- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索
39	- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合
40	- 论文章节起草
41	- 代码 review / refactor
42	
43	**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。
44	
45	## D. 汇报格式
46	
47	向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:
48	
49	    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)
50	
```

</details>
